Enzy to Enerflo issue: 

  - everytime we click on the "open in enerflo" button from Enzy, it sends us to the run title check step and starts the whole design process over again
  - the issue is that if we have a customer that we built a design for, but then they rescheduled to the next day, we have to go back in and redesign it, or find the original design in their enerflo account
  - this is an issue for if the lead was reassigned then the new closer has no idea about the pre-existing design 
  - the other issue is that if we put a link to the already made design in the enzy notes, the new closer does not have access to the previous design and has to go through all the steps again anyways 

If there was a way to just have the "open in enerflo" button go to the already made design that would be sweet
